- **process:PR-5** (minor, process), at README.md:92 (Data table, byway.sqlite row)

  "| `byway.sqlite` | Workspaces, intakes, runs, run events, checkpoints, preferences, and the spend book: ..."

  Why: ADR-0105 item 1 puts the page renderer's settings in this database: migration 8's `renderer_settings`, which holds the person's OS sandbox choice and the last start-up check, one row for the data directory. This table is the README's list of what each file holds, and it leaves them out. So a reader is not told that restoring a backup, or pointing BYWAY_DATA_DIR at another folder, also brings back that folder's sandbox setting, which may be off.

  Suggested fix: "Workspaces, intakes, runs, run events, checkpoints, preferences, the page renderer's settings (its OS sandbox switch and what the last start-up check found; [ADR-0105](docs/adr/0105-renderer-os-sandbox-on-with-an-explicit-off-switch.md)), and the spend book: ..."

  The refuter's verdict: real: yes, severity: minor.

  Reason: The finding is real but small, and its stated harm is overstated.
  
  Confirmed at a34568f:
  - The branch adds migration 8, `renderer_settings`, in `packages/store/src/migrations.ts` around lines 289-303. It holds one row for the data directory: `os_sandbox`, `changed_at` and the JSON `last_check`. ADR-0105 says the same at lines 41 and 97.
  - `git diff main fix/fx-d2 -- README.md` does not touch the Data table. README.md:92 still lists "Workspaces, intakes, runs, run events, checkpoints, preferences, and the spend book".
  - "preferences" in that row does not cover the new setting. It is the per-workspace `preferences` table from migration 3 (profile, demo mode, technical names). ADR-0105 rejects "a preference per workspace" and puts the sandbox setting at the data-directory level instead.
  - So after this branch, the README's list of what `byway.sqlite` holds leaves out a new kind of stored state, a user-facing security setting. That is a stale statement.
  
  Why it is minor, not blocking:
  1. The list was never complete. On main it already leaves out internal tables: `memos`, the `artifacts` index, `spend_faults` and `spend_book_meta`.
  2. The README already says the backup unit is the whole data directory (line 104). Restoring it bringing back the stored settings is expected.
  3. If a restored or other data directory has the sandbox off, nobody is misled for long. The README's own OS sandbox section says every screen then shows a banner and the start-up line says so too.
  4. No test checks the row's wording. `scripts/test/backup.test.ts:113` only checks that every file is named.
  
  The proposed text is accurate (the setting is the switch plus the last check's result). It also avoids the table and column names `renderer_settings` and `os_sandbox`, which `apps/web/test/guard-sandbox.test.ts` keeps to a fixed list of lines. It matches no forbidden phrase in `ci.test.ts` or `readme.test.ts`.

  Fix: File: README.md, line 92 (the `byway.sqlite` row of the Data table).
  
  Old text: "| `byway.sqlite` | Workspaces, intakes, runs, run events, checkpoints, preferences, and the spend book: every paid call"
  
  New text: "| `byway.sqlite` | Workspaces, intakes, runs, run events, checkpoints, preferences, the page renderer's settings (its OS sandbox switch and what the last start-up check found, one for the data directory; [ADR-0105](docs/adr/0105-renderer-os-sandbox-on-with-an-explicit-off-switch.md)), and the spend book: every paid call"
  
  Leave the rest of the row unchanged. Do not write the table or column names (`renderer_settings`, `os_sandbox`) in the README; guard-sandbox.test.ts keeps them to a fixed list of lines.
  
  To check it, run `pnpm vitest run scripts/test apps/web/test/guard-`.

- **process:PR-6** (minor, process), at docs/adr/0105-renderer-os-sandbox-on-with-an-explicit-off-switch.md:140-145 (Evidence), with README.md:160 and the merge itself

  ADR-0105's CI evidence cites only two runs. Run 39137471023 (at e36a0aa) ran the first script (`$HOME/.cache` only, no symbolic-link handling, no missing-folder check, no printed statement of what it allows). Run 39149469179 (at d387960) ran the reversed root-folder design. README:160 says "These steps have run on CI's Ubuntu 24.04 machine, which runs this same script before every test run".

  Why: The script as shipped (115e9db, then a3d271f) has exactly one CI run: 39172144829 at a3d271f. Read-only `gh run view` at the time of this sweep: verify (linux) success, verify (windows) still in_progress. a34568f is not pushed, but it changes only a script comment and a test regex. ADR-0103 §3 and the README's CI section say a risky change such as D2 "merges only when both jobs pass". The owner's choice of 2026-10-18T22:23Z was to merge "when the check is clean and Linux passes (Windows passes or fails only on a named timing flake)". So the record has no evidence for the design it ships, and the merge gate is still open.

  Suggested fix: Wait for run 39172144829's Windows job before merging. Add to ADR-0105's Evidence: "CI on the branch, run 39172144829 (2026-10-18, at a3d271f, the script as item 8 has it): Linux (ubuntu-24.04) passed ...; Windows ...", with the result. If Windows fails only on the named C5 flake, say there that the merge went ahead under the owner's choice of 2026-10-18. Otherwise it reads as a departure from ADR-0103 §3's "merges only when both jobs pass".

  The refuter's verdict: real: yes, severity: minor.

  Reason: The finding is real but narrower than reported. Half of it, the open merge gate, no longer applies. I checked fix/fx-d2 at a34568f.
  
  What holds: ADR-0105's Evidence names only two CI runs (lines 140 and 145). Run 39137471023, at e36a0aa, ran the first script. That script used `$HOME/.cache` only, did not follow symbolic links, did not stop when Playwright's folder is missing, did not print the `unconfined` setting, and did not print the closing statement of what it allows. Its workflow also ran the script after the font step. Run 39149469179, at d387960, ran the root-folder design, which was later reversed. `git grep` finds no mention of the run that tested the script as shipped, run 39172144829, anywhere on the branch. Line 140 says that first run's profile is "as item 8 has it again". That is roughly true, because the profile pattern is the same when `XDG_CACHE_HOME` is not set. Still, the record never cites a CI run of item 8's actual script, and every earlier design in this ADR has its run recorded. ADR-0105 is not on main (`git cat-file -e main:` fails), so it can still be edited in place.
  
  What no longer holds:
  - `gh run view 39172144829` now shows the run completed. Its commit is a3d271f82e91 and both jobs passed: verify (windows) in 40m3s and verify (linux) in 14m23s. So ADR-0103 §3's rule ("merges only when both jobs pass") is met, and the C5-flake clause in the proposed fix does not apply.
  - a34568f, which comes after that run, changes only 3 comment lines in the script and one comment and one regex in `ci.test.ts` (`git diff a3d271f a34568f`).
  - README:160 ("These steps have run on CI's Ubuntu 24.04 machine, which runs this same script before every test run") is true as written.
  
  What the Linux job's log shows:
  - The script printed "kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns = 1" and "kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_unconfined = 0".
  - It printed "Added the AppArmor profile /etc/apparmor.d/playwright-chromium: Playwright's Chromium in /home/runner/.cache/ms-playwright may start its OS sandbox now." There was no "leads to" line, so no symbolic link was involved.
  - Test Files: 198 passed (198). Tests: 4997 passed (4997). Browser tests: "55 passed (4.9m)".
  - "e2e: renderer Playwright's Chromium, ready, inside its OS sandbox".
  
  The Windows job shows 198 test files passed, "55 passed (19.7m)", and the same renderer line.
  
  Nobody is misled and nothing behaves wrongly. The gap is that the record leaves out the CI run of the design it ships, so this is minor.

  Fix: File: docs/adr/0105-renderer-os-sandbox-on-with-an-explicit-off-switch.md. The change is a new final Evidence bullet after line 154, the "Round 3's tests for the script" paragraph. Nothing else needs to change. Run 39172144829 passed on both jobs, so there is nothing to wait for, and the C5-flake sentence the finding proposed should not be added.
  
  Old text (end of line 154):
  Red runs before each fix and each new check's mutation: `C:\Users\arvik\AppData\Local\Temp\byway-fixes\scratch\d2-r3\`.
  
  New text:
  Red runs before each fix and each new check's mutation: `C:\Users\arvik\AppData\Local\Temp\byway-fixes\scratch\d2-r3\`.
  - CI on the branch, run 39172144829 (2026-10-18, at a3d271f, the script as item 8 has it; a34568f after it changes only a comment in the script and a pattern in `ci.test.ts`), both jobs green. Linux (ubuntu-24.04): the script printed "kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns = 1" and "kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_unconfined = 0" and added the profile on `/home/runner/.cache/ms-playwright`, reached through no symbolic link; 198 test files (4,997 tests) and the 55 browser tests passed, and the start-up line said "Playwright's Chromium, ready, inside its OS sandbox". Windows (windows-2025): the 198 test files and the 55 browser tests passed, with the same line.
